options reuse=yes compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO SYBASE

(SERVER   = PTASYBCTR
 DATABASE = DBCOR
 USER     = &User_PTASYBCTR.
 PASSWORD = &pw_PTASYBCTR.   );
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACORDO_00 AS
SELECT DISTINCT *
FROM CONNECTION TO SYBASE(
								SELECT DISTINCT
					              CON.DTGERACAO
					            , CON.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
					            , CON.DTVENCIMENTO
					            , CON.DTPAGAMENTO
					            , CON.STNEGOCIACAO
					            , CON.TPENTRADA
					            , CASE
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'AN' THEN 'A NEGOCIAR'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'N'  THEN 'NEGOCIADO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'PA' THEN 'PENDENTE DE APROVACAO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'A'  THEN 'APROVADO PELA BV'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'R'  THEN 'RECUSADO PELA BV'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'NA' THEN 'NEGOCIACAO EM ANDAMENTO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'C'  THEN 'NEGOCIACAO CANCELADA'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'LP' THEN 'LIBERADO PAGAMENTO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'PG' THEN 'PAGO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'PL' THEN 'PAGAMENTO REALIZADO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'DA' THEN 'DEVOLVIDO ASSESSORIA'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'CD' THEN 'CHEQUE DEVOLVIDO'
					                WHEN CON.STNEGOCIACAO = 'AC' THEN 'CHEQUE AGUARDANDO COMPENSACAO'
					             ELSE '99.VERIFICAR' END STATUS_BOLETO
						FROM DBCONTENCIOSO..TBNEGOCIACAOCONTRATO CON
						WHERE CON.DTCANCELAMENTO IS NULL

						AND CON.STNEGOCIACAO NOT IN ('R')
						AND CON.DTVENCIMENTO >  DATEADD(DD, -1, GETDATE())
						AND CON.DTVENCIMENTO <= DATEADD(DD, 15, GETDATE())
						ORDER BY CON.DTVENCIMENTO DESC) A
						Order by A.DTGERACAO DESC
;QUIT;

















































PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_ACORDO AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
, intck('DAY', DATEPART(A.DtGeracao), DATEPART(A.DtVencimento), 'C')  AS Intervalo_GerVenc_Bol
, DATEPART(A.DtGeracao) Format yymmdd10. AS Dt_Geracao_Boleto
, DATEPART(A.DtVencimento) Format yymmdd10. AS Dt_Vencimento_Boleto
, HOUR(A.DtGeracao) AS Hr_Geracao
, Case
	WHEN B.Canal_Macro = 'ESCOB' AND HOUR(A.DtGeracao) <=  7 THEN 1
	WHEN B.Canal_Macro = 'ESCOB' AND HOUR(A.DtGeracao) >= 20 THEN 1
	ELSE 0 END AS Flag_ForaExped
, B.Canal As Canal_Boleto
, B.Canal_Macro
FROM WORK.BASE_ACORDO_00 AS A
LEFT JOIN TCONFIG.CONFIG_TPENTRADA AS B ON A.TpEntrada = B.TpEntrada
Having Flag_ForaExped = 0
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_ALO_CPC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Today() Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ref
, DateTime() Format = DateTime. AS Dt_Atualizacao
, A.IdPessoa
, A.NuContrato
, A.Dt
, Max(A.TpResultado) AS Max_Resultado
, B.HashBV
, Max(A.Flag_Alo) AS Flag_Alo
, Max(A.Flag_CPC) AS Flag_CPC
FROM WORK.AUX_CPC_INTENALIZ AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_HASH AS B ON A.IdPessoa = B.IdPessoa
GROUP BY A.IdPessoa, A.NuContrato, A.Dt, B.HashBV
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_CPC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Today() Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ref
, DateTime() Format = DateTime. AS Dt_Atualizacao
, A.Dth Format = DateTime. As Dt_Ult_CPC
, A.Qtd_CPC
, A.IdPessoa
, A.NuContrato
FROM (SELECT DISTINCT MAX(Dth) AS Dth, COUNT(DISTINCT Dt) AS Qtd_CPC, IdPessoa, NuContrato
FROM WORK.AUX_CPC_INTENALIZ
WHere Flag_CPC = 1
GROUP BY IdPessoa, NuContrato) AS A
;QUIT;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_HIST_ALO AS
SELECT * FROM CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_ALO_CPC
Where Flag_Alo = 1
;QUIT;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_HIST_CPC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  MAX(A.Dt_Ref) Format = yymmdd10. AS DT_Ref
, MAX(A.Dt_Atualizacao) Format = datetime.  AS Dt_Atualizacao
, MAX(A.Dt_Ult_CPC) Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ult_CPC
, MAX(A.Qtd_CPC) AS Qtd_CPC
, A.IdPessoa
, A.NuContrato
FROM (
SELECT DISTINCT A.Dt_Ref, A.Dt_Atualizacao, DATEPART(A.Dt_Ult_CPC) Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ult_CPC, A.Qtd_CPC, A.IdPessoa, A.NuContrato FROM CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_CPC AS A

UNION ALL

SELECT A.Dt_Ref, A.Dt_Atualizacao, A.Dt Format = yymmdd10. As Dt_Ult_CPC, 1 AS Qtd_CPC, A.IdPessoa, A.NuContrato FROM CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_ALO_CPC AS A
Where A.Flag_CPC = 1) AS A
GROUP BY A.IdPessoa, A.NuContrato
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.PERF_AMIGAVEL AS
SELECT *
FROM CART_HST.PERF_AMIGAVEL
WHERE SAFRA >= INTNX('MONTH',TODAY(),-3)
AND (YEAR(SAFRA)*100+MONTH(SAFRA)) <> (YEAR(TODAY())*100+MONTH(TODAY()))
AND DIAS_ATRASO >= 5
 
;QUIT;










PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RECORR_ATRASO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  SAFRA
, CHAVE
, COUNT(DISTINCT CHAVE) AS QTD
FROM WORK.PERF_AMIGAVEL
GROUP BY SAFRA, CHAVE
ORDER BY CHAVE DESC
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RECORR_ATRASO_FINAL AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  CHAVE
, SUM(QTD) AS QTD
FROM WORK.RECORR_ATRASO
GROUP BY CHAVE
Having QTD >= 2 
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;

;QUIT;

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME CONTATOS '/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/contatos';




options reuse=yes compress=binary;





PROC SQL;
  CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM
    (project='bv-sadi-dtm'
     credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
     schema='bv-pdbd-prd'
     read_mode=storage);

  CREATE TABLE WORK.LINHA_DIGITAVEL AS
  SELECT DISTINCT
  Today() Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ref
, Datetime() Format = e8601dt19. Informat = e8601dt19. AS Dth_Atualizacao
, Input(A.nucontrato,best14.) Format = best14. Informat = best14. AS NuContrato
, A.sqcontratofinanceiro
, A.nuparcela
, A.dtvencimento Format = yymmdd10. As dtvencimento
, A.vrtitulo AS Valor_Original_Parcela
, A.Dias_Vencimento
, A.nudigitavelcodigobarra
, A.fldebitoautomatico
, A.dsqrcodepagamentopix
From connection to PERM (Select Distinct
  nucontrato
, sqcontratofinanceiro
, nuparcela
, dtvencimento
, DATE_DIFF(current_date(), dtvencimento, DAY) AS Dias_Vencimento
, vrtitulo
, nudigitavelcodigobarra
, fldebitoautomatico
, dsqrcodepagamentopix
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbgopr_goprregistroparcial.tbsolicitacaoregistroparcial`
WHERE flativo = 'S'
AND nuparcela IS NOT NULL
AND dtvencimento Between CAST(CURRENT_DATE()-15 AS DATE) AND CAST(CURRENT_DATE()+3 AS DATE)
) AS A;
disconnect from PERM;
quit;
































































































Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Dth_Atualizacao
, B.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATO
, A.SqContratoFinanceiro
, A.NUPARCELA
, A.Dias_Vencimento AS ATRASO
, A.Valor_Original_Parcela
, A.DTVENCIMENTO Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Vencimento_Original
, B.QtdParcAtraso AS Qtd_Parcelas_Atraso
, A.NuDigitavelCodigoBarra
, (A.DTVENCIMENTO+15) FORMAT DDMMYY10. AS Dt_Max_Pgto
FROM WORK.LINHA_DIGITAVEL AS A
INNER JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_IQ_CARTEIRA AS B ON A.NUCONTRATO = B.NUCONTRATO AND A.SqContratoFinanceiro = B.SqContratoFinanceiro AND A.NuParcela = B.NuParcela
WHERE A.Dias_Vencimento <= 15

; QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.VALID_5A15 AS
SELECT DISTINCT NUCONTRATO, COUNT(*) AS QTD FROM WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15 GROUP BY NUCONTRATO ORDER BY QTD DESC;QUIT;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_LINHADIG_5A15 AS
SELECT DISTINCT * FROM WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
UPDATE CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER
SET Flag_LinhaDig = 1
Where NuContratoGestao IN (Select NUCONTRATO FROM WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15)
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RATINHO_ATIVA AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Monotonic() AS Ordem
, A.*
FROM WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15 AS A
HAVING Ordem = 1
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_OFERTA_RODAS_5A15 AS
SELECT A.* FROM WORK.OFERTA_RODAS_5A15 AS A
UNION ALL
SELECT DISTINCT
  B.DT_REF
, B.Dth_Atualizacao
, C.NUCPFCNPJ
, C.NUCONTRATO_RODAS AS NUCONTRATO
, B.SqContratoFinanceiro
, B.NUPARCELA
, B.ATRASO
, B.Valor_Original_Parcela
, B.Dt_Vencimento_Original
, B.Qtd_Parcelas_Atraso
, B.NuDigitavelCodigoBarra
, B.Dt_Max_Pgto
FROM WORK.RATINHO_ATIVA AS B, RATINHO.Ratinho_Unificado AS C
Where C.Flag_Ativo = 1
AND C.Flag_CPF = 1
AND C.RG_RodasProp_Digital = 1
;QUIT;


PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.BASE_OFERTA_RODAS_5A15
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/BASE_OFERTA_RODAS_5A15_&DT_ARQ..csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;



Options reuse=yes compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_OFERTA_RODAS_5A15_QQ AS
SELECT A.*
, B.Telefone_Completo
, C.Email
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_LINHADIG_5A15     AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.IDPESSOA
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_EMAIL    AS C ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = C.IDPESSOA
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER   AS D ON A.NUCONTRATO = D.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
WHERE D.GRUPO = 'RODAS'
AND (D.FLGFALEC = 'N' OR D.FLGFALEC IS MISSING)
AND (D.FLGSUSP = 'N' OR D.FLGSUSP IS MISSING)
AND B.Telefone_Completo IS NOT MISSING
AND C.Email IS NOT MISSING
AND D.Flag_D0 = 'N'
AND D.ATRASO <= 15
AND D.COD_ASSESSORIA IN (1627, 1628, 2062)
AND INPUT(SUBSTR(A.NUCPFCNPJ,7,2),best2.) BETWEEN 00 AND 49
;QUIT;


PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.BASE_OFERTA_RODAS_5A15_QQ
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/mktplace//QUEROQUITAR_&DT_ARQ._5A15_INCLUSAO_ENVIO.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_DU AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Data Format = Yymmdd10. AS Dt
, Du
, Periodo
, FlFeriado
FROM AUX_DU.BASE_AUX_DU
;QUIT;





options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO sasiosiq AS dbcon
( Host     = "iqprdbi"
  Server   = iqprdbi
  Db       = iqprdbi
  User     = &User_IQPRDBI.
  Password = &pw_IQPRDBI.   );
CREATE TABLE WORK.FATURA_CARTAO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  TODAY() FORMAT yymmdd10. AS DT_REF
, DATETIME() Format datetime. AS Ult_Atualizacao
, *
FROM CONNECTION TO DBCON (



SELECT DISTINCT
  A.CdContaCartao
, A.DtVencimentoFatura
, A.CdFatura
, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura
, A.Safra
, Max(B.DtCorteFatura) As DtCorteFatura
, Max(B.CdLinhaDigitavelFatura) as Linha_Digitavel
, MAX(B.VrPagamentoMinimoFatura) AS Vlr_Minimo
, MAX(B.VrSaldoFatura) AS Vlr_SaldoFatura
INTO #Base_Fatura
FROM (SELECT DISTINCT
  A.CdContaCartao
, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura
, (Year(A.DtVencimentoFatura)*100+Month(A.DtVencimentoFatura)) AS Safra
, Max(A.DtVencimentoFatura) As DtVencimentoFatura
, Max(A.CdFatura) AS CdFatura
FROM DWBI_DTWH.TbFatura AS A
INNER JOIN DWBI_DMOC.TbFatoFatura AS B ON A.CdFatura = B.CdFatura
WHERE A.DtVencimentoFatura >= getdate()-30
AND B.FlFaturaEmitida = 'S'
AND B.FlFaturaDesconsiderada ='N'
GROUP BY A.CDCONTACARTAO, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura, (Year(A.DtVencimentoFatura)*100+Month(A.DtVencimentoFatura))) AS A
INNER JOIN DWBI_DTWH.TbFatura AS B ON A.DtVencimentoFatura = B.DtVencimentoFatura AND A.CdContaCartao = B.CdContaCartao
WHERE B.CdLinhaDigitavelFatura IS NOT NULL
AND B.CdLinhaDigitavelFatura NOT IN (' ')
GROUP BY A.CdContaCartao, A.DtVencimentoFatura, A.CdFatura, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura, A.Safra






SELECT DISTINCT
  A.CdContaCartao
, A.CdFatura
, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura
, A.Linha_Digitavel
, A.DtCorteFatura
, A.DtVencimentoFatura
, A.Vlr_Minimo
, A.Vlr_SaldoFatura
, B.CdTipoParcelado
, C.NmTipoParcelado
, CASE WHEN B.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra > 800 THEN (B.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra - 800) ELSE B.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra  END AS CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra
, B.VrParcelaEntrada
, B.VrDemaisParcelas
FROM #Base_Fatura AS A
LEFT JOIN DWBI_DTWH.TbFaturaOfertaParcelamento AS B ON A.CdFatura = B.CdFatura
LEFT JOIN DWBI_DTWH.TbTipoParcelado AS C ON B.CdTipoParcelado = C.CdTipoParcelado AND C.NmTipoParcelado = 'PARCELADO CONVENCIONAL'
WHERE A.Linha_Digitavel IS NOT NULL)
;QUIT;


Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
, INPUT(A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura,best13.) Format = Best10. Informat = Best10.  AS CdNossoNumeroBolFatura
, DATEPART(DtVencimentoFatura) Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_VencFatura
, CASE
	WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.))) = 10 THEN INPUT(PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.)),1,1),best3.),z3.) || PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.)),2,9),best10.),z10.),best14.)
	WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.))) = 11 THEN INPUT(PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.)),1,2),best3.),z3.) || PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(PUT(A.CdContaCartao,11.)),3,9),best10.),z10.),best14.)
ELSE . END Format = best14. Informat = best14. AS NuContratoGestao
FROM WORK.FATURA_CARTAO AS A
INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT CdContaCartao, MAX(DtCorteFatura) AS DtCorteFatura FROM WORK.FATURA_CARTAO GROUP BY CdContaCartao) AS B ON A.CdContaCartao = B.CdContaCartao AND A.DtCorteFatura = B.DtCorteFatura

;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
FROM WORK.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS A
;QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: Db_Fat_Cartao */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 036 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: DB_Bloq_Cartao_CNAB */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 037 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 147 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: DB_Bloq_Cartao_CNAB */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-2xYPMLKN8v2QWVGj/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_CNAB_CART_TRAT AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Ult_Atualizacao
, A.CdContaCartao
, A.CdFatura
, A.CdNossoNumeroBoletoFatura
, A.Linha_Digitavel
, A.DtCorteFatura
, A.DtVencimentoFatura
, A.Vlr_Minimo
, A.Vlr_SaldoFatura
, A.CdNossoNumeroBolFatura
, A.Dt_VencFatura
, A.NuContratoGestao
FROM WORK.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS A
INNER JOIN WORK.AUX_CNAB_CART AS B ON A.CdNossoNumeroBolFatura = B.NossoNumeroBV AND A.Dt_VencFatura = B.DtVencimentoTitulo
WHERE B.DtOcorrencia >= INTNX('DAY',TODAY(),-3)
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
UPDATE CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER
SET Flag_Acordo = 'S'
Where NuContratoGestao IN (SELECT DISTINCT NuContratoGestao FROM WORK.AUX_CNAB_CART_TRAT )
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
UPDATE CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER
SET Flag_D0 = 'S'
Where NuContratoGestao IN (SELECT DISTINCT NuContratoGestao FROM WORK.AUX_CNAB_CART_TRAT )
;QUIT;

/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */
/* TASK: DB_Bloq_Cartao_CNAB */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 037 */
/* =========================FIM TASK=========================== */

/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
/* TASK: DB_Matriz_Whats */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 038 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 147 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: DB_Matriz_Whats */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-oJPOEhJpMgeCtIcX/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;




DATA _NULL_ ;
CALL SYMPUT('Hr_Arq', (PUT(HOUR(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)||PUT(MINUTE(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)));
RUN;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_INFO_VEIC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, CASE WHEN A.VEIC_PLACA IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE CATS(SUBSTR(A.VEIC_PLACA,1,2),'xxx',SUBSTR(A.VEIC_PLACA,6,2)) END AS PLACA
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_CYBER_INFO_VEIC AS A
WHERE A.VEIC_PLACA IS NOT MISSING
ORDER BY A.NUCPFCNPJ
;QUIT;




Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;




PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, CASE WHEN A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ AND B.QTD > 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_MultiplosContr
, CASE
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' AND A.SISTEMA = 'GCON' THEN 'SOLAR GCON'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' AND A.SISTEMA = 'FLEX' THEN 'SOLAR FLEX'

WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' AND A.SISTEMA = 'GCON' THEN 'SOLAR GCON'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' AND A.SISTEMA = 'FLEX' THEN 'SOLAR FLEX'

ELSE A.GRUPO END AS PRODUTO
, PROPCASE(CASE
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN A.Flag_TipoContr
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SALDO' THEN 'Saldo Remanescente'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' THEN (COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) || ' placa Solar')
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' THEN (COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) || ' placa Solar')
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' THEN (COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) || ' placa Solar')
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'RODAS' AND COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) IS MISSING THEN 'financiamento veículo'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP' AND A.CARTEIRA = 'NEON' THEN 'empréstimo crédito pessoal BV NEON'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP' AND COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) IS MISSING THEN 'empréstimo crédito pessoal'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CP','RODAS','CONSIG') THEN (COMPRESS(A.Flag_TipoContr) ||' '|| TRIM(A.Flag_TipoProd))
ELSE 'Verificar' END) AS Tipo_Contrato
, CASE WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN (COMPRESS(A.BANDEIRA_CARTAO)||' '||COMPRESS(A.VARIANTE_CARTAO))
   WHEN A.GRUPO = 'RODAS' THEN C.PLACA
   WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SALDO' THEN C.PLACA
   WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CP'   THEN 'final '|| SUBSTR(PUT(A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO,z14.),10,5)
   WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' THEN 'final '|| SUBSTR(PUT(A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO,z14.),10,5)
   WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CONSIG' THEN 'final '|| SUBSTR(PUT(A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO,z14.),10,5)
   ELSE '' END AS Detalhe_Contrato
, CASE
WHEN A.ATRASO <= 0 THEN 'MATRIZ_INAD'


WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'MATRIZ_INAD'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 5 AND 15 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_FINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO >= 16 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_FINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CA') AND A.ATRASO <= 94 THEN 'MATRIZ_INAD'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CA') AND A.ATRASO >= 94 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_CARTAO_CL'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CL') AND A.ATRASO >= 95 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_CARTAO_CL'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CARTAO') AND A.ATRASO <= 94 THEN 'MATRIZ_INAD' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CARTAO') AND A.ATRASO >= 95 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_CARTAO_CL' 

WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CP') AND A.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'MATRIZ_INAD'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CP') AND A.ATRASO >= 5 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_FINANCEIRA' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('SOLAR') AND A.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'WHATS3003' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('SOLAR') AND A.ATRASO >= 5 THEN 'NEGOCIACAO_FINANCEIRA' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CONSIG') AND A.ATRASO <= 15 THEN 'SEM_NEG'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CONSIG') AND A.ATRASO >= 16 THEN 'SEM_NEG'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SALDO' THEN 'SALDO'
ELSE 'Verificar' END AS FONTE_OFERTA
, CASE
WHEN A.ATRASO <= 0 AND A. Flag_PendGravame = 'S' THEN 'REGULARIZAGRAVAME'
WHEN A.ATRASO <= 0 THEN 'ADIMPLENTE'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.COD_ASSESSORIA IN (2062, 2063, 2064, 2065, 2066, 2067, 2068) THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO <   5 THEN 'ADIMPLENTE'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 5 AND 15 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CA') AND A.ATRASO <= 94 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOCARTAOCA'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CA') AND A.ATRASO >= 95 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOCARTAOCL'
WHEN A.CARTEIRA IN ('CARTAO_CL') AND A.ATRASO >= 95 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOCARTAOCL'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CARTAO') AND A.ATRASO <= 75 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOCARTAOCA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CARTAO') AND A.ATRASO >= 76 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOCARTAOCL'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('RODAS') AND A.ATRASO >= 16 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CP') AND A.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'WHATS3003' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CP') AND A.ATRASO >= 5 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('SOLAR') AND A.ATRASO < 5 THEN 'WHATS3003' 
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('SOLAR') AND A.ATRASO >= 5 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CONSIG') AND A.ATRASO <= 15 THEN 'BASEOFERTA5A15'
WHEN A.GRUPO IN ('CONSIG') AND A.ATRASO >= 16 THEN 'NEGOCIACAOFINANCEIRA'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'SALDO' THEN 'SALDO'
ELSE 'Verificar' END AS GRUPO_OFERTA
, D.Telefone_Completo
, A.ATRASO
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN (SELECT NUCPFCNPJ, COUNT(DISTINCT NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER GROUP BY NUCPFCNPJ) AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ
LEFT JOIN WORK.AUX_INFO_VEIC AS C ON A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = C.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE AS D ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = D.IDPESSOA
WHERE A.GRUPO NOT IN ('SALDO','CONSIG')
Having FONTE_OFERTA NOT IN ('Verificar')
ORDER BY Flag_MultiplosContr DESC, A.NUCPFCNPJ ASC
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RESUMO_MATRIZ AS
SELECT DISTINCT DT_REF, PRODUTO, FONTE_OFERTA, GRUPO_OFERTA, COUNT(NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD_CONTR
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01
GROUP BY DT_REF, PRODUTO, FONTE_OFERTA, GRUPO_OFERTA
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RESUMO_MATRIZ_01 AS
SELECT DISTINCT DT_REF, PRODUTO, Tipo_Contrato, FONTE_OFERTA, GRUPO_OFERTA, COUNT(NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD_CONTR
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01
GROUP BY DT_REF, PRODUTO, Tipo_Contrato, FONTE_OFERTA, GRUPO_OFERTA
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE RESUMO_MATRIZ_02 AS
SELECT DISTINCT DT_REF, PRODUTO, Tipo_Contrato, GRUPO_OFERTA, COUNT(NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD_CONTR, Datetime() format = e8601dt19. As Dth_Ini_Processo_Arq, intnx('minute',Datetime(),15) Format = e8601dt19. As Dth_Fim_Processo_Arq
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01
GROUP BY DT_REF, PRODUTO, Tipo_Contrato, GRUPO_OFERTA
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.VALIDA_RESUMO AS
SELECT * FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01
WHERE PRODUTO = 'RODAS'
AND Tipo_Contrato IN ('Empréstimo Consignado','Empréstimo Crédito Pessoal')

UNION ALL

SELECT * FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01
WHERE PRODUTO = 'SOLAR GCON'
AND Tipo_Contrato IN ('Empréstimo Placa Solar','Empréstimo Crédito Pessoal')
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_OFERTA_CARTAO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, PROPCASE(A.PRI_NOME) AS NOME_CLIENTE
, PROPCASE(A.BANDEIRA_CARTAO) AS BANDEIRA
, Propcase(A.VARIANTE_CARTAO) AS VARIANTE
, B.Vlr_SaldoFatura AS VALOR_TOTAL
, B.Vlr_Minimo AS VALOR_MINIMO
, DATEPART(B.DtCorteFatura) FORMAT DDMMYY10. As Dt_CorteFatura
, (A.DT_REF - A.ATRASO) FORMAT DDMMYY10. AS Dt_Vencimento_Original
, A.ATRASO
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MaxPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, CASE WHEN B.Max_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Max_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MedPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, CASE WHEN B.Med_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Med_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MinPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, CASE WHEN B.Min_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Min_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, B.Linha_Digitavel

, INTNX('DAY', DATEPART(B.DtCorteFatura),27) FORMAT DDMMYY10. AS Dt_Max_Pgto
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_IQ_OFERTA_PARC_FATURA AS B ON A.CdContaCartaoIQ = B.CdContaCartao
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_CARTAO_VARIANTE AS C ON A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = C.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
WHERE  A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO'
AND A.ATRASO <= 95

AND INTNX('DAY', DATEPART(B.DtCorteFatura), 27)
;QUIT;



















Options reuse=yes compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.MATRIZ_INAD AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.Flag_MultiplosContr
, A.PRODUTO
, A.Tipo_Contrato
, A.Detalhe_Contrato
, A.FONTE_OFERTA
, A.GRUPO_OFERTA
, A.Telefone_Completo
, CASE
WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContrato AND A.PRODUTO IN ('RODAS','CP') THEN C.Dt_Vencimento_Original
WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao AND A.PRODUTO = 'CARTAO' THEN B.Dt_Vencimento_Original
  ELSE (A.DT_REF - A.ATRASO) END AS Dt_Vencimento_Original Format = DDMMYY10.
, CASE
WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContrato AND A.PRODUTO IN ('RODAS','CP') THEN C.ATRASO
WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao AND A.PRODUTO = 'CARTAO' THEN B.ATRASO
  ELSE A.ATRASO END AS ATRASO
, CASE
WHEN A.PRODUTO IN ('RODAS','CP') THEN C.NuDigitavelCodigoBarra
WHEN A.PRODUTO = 'CARTAO' THEN B.Linha_Digitavel
  ELSE '' END AS Linha_Digitavel
, CASE
WHEN A.PRODUTO IN ('RODAS','CP') THEN C.Dt_Max_Pgto
WHEN A.PRODUTO = 'CARTAO' THEN B.Dt_Max_Pgto
  ELSE . END AS Dt_Max_Pgto Format = DDMMYY10.
, B.VARIANTE
, B.VALOR_TOTAL AS Vlr_Total_Fatura
, B.VALOR_MINIMO AS Vlr_Minimo_Fatura
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, C.NUPARCELA
, C.Valor_Original_Parcela
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD_01 AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.AUX_OFERTA_CARTAO AS B ON A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_LINHADIG_5A15 AS C ON A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContrato
;QUIT;

PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.MATRIZ_INAD
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/zzzz_&DT_ARQ._OLD_MATRIZ_IND.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;




































 
 
 


 
 
 


 
 
 




















Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.QUERO_QUITAR_CA AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  C.Telefone_Completo AS TELEFONE
, B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO  AS CONTRATO Format= best14.
, B.NUCPFCNPJ AS CPF
, B.NOME_CLIENTE
, B.BANDEIRA
, B.VARIANTE
, B.Dt_CorteFatura AS DATA_CORTE Format=DDMMYY10.
, B.Dt_Vencimento_Original AS DATA_VENCIMENTO Format=DDMMYY10.
, B.VALOR_TOTAL
, B.VALOR_MINIMO
, B.Dt_Vencimento_Original AS DATA_PRI_ATRASO Format=DDMMYY10.
, B.Atraso
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, B.Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, B.Linha_Digitavel
, B.Dt_Max_Pgto
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
INNER JOIN WORK.AUX_OFERTA_CARTAO AS B ON A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = B.NuContratoGestao
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE AS C ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = C.IDPESSOA
WHERE A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO'
AND A.COD_ASSESSORIA = 4010
AND A.ATRASO >= 3
AND A.Flag_D0 = 'N'
AND A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO NOT IN (SELECT DISTINCT D.NUCONTRATOGESTAO FROM WORK.AUX_CNAB_CART_TRAT AS D)
AND INPUT(SUBSTR(A.NUCPFCNPJ,7,2),best2.) BETWEEN 50 AND 74
;QUIT;

PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.QUERO_QUITAR_CA
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/mktplace/QUEROQUITAR_&DT_ARQ._CARTAOCA_INCLUSAO_ENVIO.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;

Options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_TEL_BLOQ_RFLOW AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.DT_REF, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO, A.NUCPFCNPJ, B.DDD, B.Telefone
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.IDPESSOA
WHERE A.NUCPFCNPJ NOT IN (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ FROM (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ, COUNT(DISTINCT NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD_CONTR FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER GROUP BY NUCPFCNPJ Having QTD_CONTR > 1))
AND B.Telefone IS NOT MISSING
ORDER BY A.NUCPFCNPJ  ASC
;QUIT;

PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.AUX_TEL_BLOQ_RFLOW
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/&DT_ARQ._AUX_TEL_BLOQ_RFLOW.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;










































Options Reuse=Yes Compress=Binary;





































































































































































Options Reuse=Yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.INTEGRACAO_RFLOW AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, DATETIME() Format = e8601dt19. AS Dt_Atualizacao
, A.NuContratoGestao Format = Best14. AS NuContrato
, CASE
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('BELLINATI') THEN 'BELLINATI'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('CONTROLE_CYBER') THEN 'BV'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('NO_ACTION') THEN 'BV'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('CONCILIG') THEN 'CONCILIG'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('CRC') THEN 'CRC'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('LM_ANGELIS') THEN 'LM_ANGELIS'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('OLIVEIRA') THEN 'OLIVEIRA'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('PASCHOALOTTO') THEN 'PASCHOALOTTO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('PASQUALI') THEN 'PASQUALI'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('SCHULZE') THEN 'SCHULZE'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('FLEX') THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('SERVICES') THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('TOLEDO') THEN 'TOLEDO'
WHEN A.ASSESSORIA IN ('MANDALITI') THEN 'MANDALITI'
ELSE 'BV' END AS Assessoria




, CASE
WHEN A.FLGSUSP = 'S' THEN 1
WHEN A.Flag_D0 = 'S' THEN 1
ELSE 0 END AS Bloqueio_Contrato
, CASE WHEN A.UF = B.UF AND A.Cidade = B.Municipio AND B.DT_FERIADO = TODAY() THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Feriado_Municipal
, CASE
 
 
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'RODAS'  
AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 16 AND 720
AND A.COD_ASSESSORIA NOT IN (2017, 2018, 1589, 1588, 1606, 1590, 1591, 2020, 1621, 2041, 2052, 1581, 1584) THEN '600'
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' AND A.ATRASO <=95 THEN '600'
ELSE '1200'
END AS Excecao_Renitencia
, CASE
  
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'RODAS'
AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 16 AND 720
AND A.COD_ASSESSORIA NOT IN (2017, 2018, 1589, 1588, 1606, 1590, 1591, 2020, 1621, 2041, 2052, 1581, 1584) THEN '25'  
WHEN A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 60 AND 95 THEN '25'
ELSE ''
END AS Excecao_Spin
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN TCONFIG.CONFIG_FERIADO_MUNICIPAL AS B ON A.UF = B.UF AND A.Cidade = B.Municipio AND B.DT_FERIADO = TODAY()
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;








DATA _NULL_ ;
CALL SYMPUT('Hr_Arq', (PUT(HOUR(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)||PUT(MINUTE(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)));
RUN;

PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.INTEGRACAO_RFLOW
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/&DT_ARQ._&Hr_Arq._2_INTEGRACAO_FLOW_DNCLBV.dat"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO_INTEGRACAO_RFLOW AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  DT_REF
, Dt_Atualizacao
, Assessoria
, COUNT(NuContrato) AS Qtd_Contr
, SUM(Bloqueio_Contrato) AS Bloqueio_Contrato
, SUM(Feriado_Municipal) AS Feriado_Municipal
, SUM(CASE WHEN Excecao_Renitencia IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Excecao_Renitencia
, SUM(CASE WHEN Excecao_Spin IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Excecao_Spin
FROM WORK.INTEGRACAO_RFLOW
GROUP BY DT_REF, Dt_Atualizacao, Assessoria
;QUIT;


PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.RESUMO_INTEGRACAO_RFLOW
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/&DT_ARQ._1_RESUMO_INTEGRACAO_RFLOW.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;








Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO_MATRIZ_BV AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.PRODUTO
, A.Tipo_Contrato
, A.FONTE_OFERTA
, A.GRUPO_OFERTA
, COUNT(A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD AS A
GROUP BY A.DT_REF
, A.PRODUTO
, A.Tipo_Contrato
, A.FONTE_OFERTA
, A.GRUPO_OFERTA
;QUIT;

options reuse=yes compress=binary;

LIBNAME CONTATOS '/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/contatos';
LIBNAME DESDOBRO 	'/riscosbv/cobranca/csaure/Piloto_Reneg_cartao_Veiculo/';




PROC SQL;
SELECT CATX('.', A.LIBNAME, A.MEMNAME)
INTO :tab_desdobro_lib SEPARATED BY ' '
FROM DICTIONARY.TABLES AS A
WHERE A.LIBNAME = 'DESDOBRO'
AND A.MEMNAME = (SELECT DISTINCT ('BASE_ELEGIVEL_'||PUT(MAX(INPUT(SUBSTR(A.MEMNAME,15,8),BEST8.)),8.)) AS TABELA
	  FROM DICTIONARY.TABLES AS A
	  WHERE A.LIBNAME = 'DESDOBRO'
	  AND A.MEMNAME LIKE 'BASE_ELEGIVEL_202%')
;QUIT;




%put &tab_desdobro_lib;
DATA BASE_DESDOBRO;
	SET &tab_desdobro_lib.;
RUN;




Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_DESDOBRO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.dtgeracao
, A.nucontrato
, A.nucontratogestao
, CASE WHEN A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO AND B.flag_elegibilidade = 'elegível-Postergar' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_ElegPostergar
FROM WORK.BASE_DESDOBRO 					AS A
LEFT JOIN DESDOBRO.BA_ELEG_POSTERG_RODAS 	AS B ON A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO = B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO AND B.flag_elegibilidade = 'elegível-Postergar'
WHERE A.teste_controle = 'Teste'
And A.dtProximoVencimentov >= Today()
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_00 AS
SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ
, CASE
	WHEN CARTEIRA = 'SOLAR' 	THEN 'SOLAR'
	WHEN GRUPO 	  = 'SALDO' 	THEN 'OUTROS'
	WHEN GRUPO    = 'CONSIG' 	THEN 'OUTROS'
ELSE GRUPO END AS GRUPO
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC TRANSPOSE DATA=WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_00 OUT=WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_01 PREFIX=PROD;
  BY NOTSORTED NUCPFCNPJ;
  VAR GRUPO;
RUN;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_02 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  NUCPFCNPJ
, TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|") AS Group_Prod
, CASE

WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|' 					THEN 'Cartao'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|'      				THEN 'CP'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'OUTRO|'       			THEN 'Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'RODAS|'       			THEN 'Rodas'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'SOLAR|'      				THEN 'Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|'      				THEN 'Cartao_CP'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|OUTROS|'      			THEN 'Cartao_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|RODAS|'      			THEN 'Cartao_Rodas'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|SOLAR|'      			THEN 'Cartao_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|OUTROS|'      				THEN 'CP_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|RODAS|'      				THEN 'Rodas_CP'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'OUTROS|RODAS|'      			THEN 'Rodas_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'RODAS|SOLAR|'      			THEN 'Rodas_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|SOLAR|'      				THEN 'Solar_CP'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'OUTROS|SOLAR|'      			THEN 'Solar_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|OUTROS|'      		THEN 'Cartao_CP_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|SOLAR|'      		THEN 'Cartao_CP_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|OUTROS|SOLAR|'      	THEN 'Cartao_Solar_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|RODAS|'      		THEN 'Rodas_Cartao_CP'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|OUTROS|RODAS|'      	THEN 'Rodas_Cartao_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|RODAS|SOLAR|'      	THEN 'Rodas_Cartao_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|RODAS|SOLAR|'      		THEN 'Rodas_CP_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|OUTROS|RODAS'      	THEN 'Rodas_Cartao_CP_Outros'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CARTAO|CP|RODAS|SOLAR'      	THEN 'Rodas_Cartao_CP_Solar'
WHEN TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|")  = 'CP|OUTROS|RODAS|'				THEN 'Rodas_CP_Outros'
ELSE TRANWRD(TRANWRD(COMPRESS(PROD1||"|"||PROD2||"|"||PROD3||"|"||PROD4),"|||","|"),"||","|") END AS Group_Produto
FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_01
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_03 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.NUCPFCNPJ
, COUNT(DISTINCT B.NUCONTRATOGESTAO) 	AS Qtd_Contr
, COUNT(DISTINCT A.GRUPO) 				AS Qtd_Grupo
FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_00  AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ
GROUP BY A.NUCPFCNPJ
ORDER BY 2 DESC
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_04 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, CASE
	WHEN A.CARTEIRA 	= 'SOLAR' 	THEN 'SOLAR'
	WHEN A.GRUPO 	 	= 'SALDO' 	THEN 'OUTROS'
	WHEN A.GRUPO    	= 'CONSIG' 	THEN 'OUTROS'
ELSE A.GRUPO END AS GRUPO
 , A.CARTEIRA
, CASE WHEN A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_Contr
, CASE
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 THEN 'Puro'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 THEN 'Multiplo'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo > 1 THEN 'MultiProd'
Else 'Verificar' END AS Agrupamento_Cliente
, CASE
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'SALDO'  THEN 'Outros'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CONSIG' THEN 'Outros'

	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'RODAS' 	THEN 'Rodas'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN 'Cartao'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' 	THEN 'Solar'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CP' 	THEN 'Emprestimos'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr = 1 						THEN  C.Group_Produto

	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'RODAS' 	THEN 'Rodas'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN 'Cartao'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'SOLAR' 	THEN 'Solar'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CP' 	THEN 'Emprestimos'

	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'SALDO' 	THEN 'Outros'
	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 AND A.GRUPO = 'CONSIG' THEN 'Outros'

	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo = 1 THEN  A.GRUPO

	WHEN B.Qtd_Contr > 1 AND B.Qtd_Grupo > 1 THEN C.Group_Produto
Else 'Verificar' END AS Agrupamento_Contr
, B.Qtd_Contr
, B.Qtd_Grupo
, C.Group_Produto
, A.NuParcela
, A.Dt_Vcto_Parcela
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER	 AS A
LEFT JOIN AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_03 AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ
LEFT JOIN AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_02 AS C ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = C.NUCPFCNPJ
ORDER BY 2 DESC
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_05 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Agrupamento_Cliente
, Agrupamento_Contr
, SUM(Flag_Contr) AS Qtd_Contr
FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_04
GROUP BY Agrupamento_Cliente, Agrupamento_Contr
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_05 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.Agrupamento_Cliente
, A.Agrupamento_Contr
, SUM(A.Flag_Contr) AS Qtd_Contr
, MAX(B.ATRASO) AS ATRASO
, MAX(B.Flag_HU) AS Flag_HU
, MAX(B.Flag_HU_3_6) AS Flag_HU_3_6
, MIN(CASE
WHEN B.Collection = 'SEM_MOD' THEN 4
WHEN B.Collection = 'BOM' THEN 3
WHEN B.Collection = 'REGULAR' THEN 2
WHEN B.Collection = 'RUIM' THEN 1
ELSE 0 END) AS Collection
, MAX(CASE WHEN B.COD_ASSESSORIA IN (1627,1628) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Flag_100Dig
FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_04 AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS B ON A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao
GROUP BY A.NUCPFCNPJ, A.Agrupamento_Cliente, A.Agrupamento_Contr
ORDER BY 1, 2
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_06 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.Agrupamento_Cliente
, A.Agrupamento_Contr
, CASE
WHEN A.Collection = 4 THEN 'SEM_MOD'
WHEN A.Collection = 3 THEN 'BOM'
WHEN A.Collection = 2 THEN 'REGULAR'
WHEN A.Collection = 1 THEN 'RUIM'
ELSE 'SEM_MOD' END AS Collection
, CASE
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN    0 AND    4 THEN '0001 - 0004'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN    5 AND   15 THEN '0005 - 0015'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   16 AND   30 THEN '0016 - 0030'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   31 AND   45 THEN '0031 - 0045'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   46 AND   70 THEN '0046 - 0070'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   71 AND  120 THEN '0071 - 0120'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  121 AND  150 THEN '0121 - 0150'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  151 AND  180 THEN '0151 - 0180'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  181 AND  360 THEN '0181 - 0360'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  361 AND  540 THEN '0361 - 0540'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  541 AND  720 THEN '0541 - 0720'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  721 AND 1080 THEN '0721 - 1080'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN 1081 AND 1800 THEN '1081 - 1800'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN 1801 AND 3600 THEN '1801 - 3600'
WHEN A.ATRASO > 	  3600 		    THEN '3601 - 9999'
ELSE PUT(A.ATRASO,best.) END AS FX_Atraso
, SUM(A.Qtd_Contr) AS Qtd_Contr
, SUM(A.Flag_HU) AS Flag_HU
, SUM(A.Flag_HU_3_6) AS Flag_HU_3_6
, SUM(A.Flag_100Dig) AS Flag_100Dig
FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_05 AS A
GROUP BY A.Agrupamento_Cliente
, A.Agrupamento_Contr
, CASE
WHEN A.Collection = 4 THEN 'SEM_MOD'
WHEN A.Collection = 3 THEN 'BOM'
WHEN A.Collection = 2 THEN 'REGULAR'
WHEN A.Collection = 1 THEN 'RUIM'
ELSE 'SEM_MOD' END
, CASE
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN    0 AND    4 THEN '0001 - 0004'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN    5 AND   15 THEN '0005 - 0015'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   16 AND   30 THEN '0016 - 0030'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   31 AND   45 THEN '0031 - 0045'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   46 AND   70 THEN '0046 - 0070'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN   71 AND  120 THEN '0071 - 0120'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  121 AND  150 THEN '0121 - 0150'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  151 AND  180 THEN '0151 - 0180'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  181 AND  360 THEN '0181 - 0360'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  361 AND  540 THEN '0361 - 0540'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  541 AND  720 THEN '0541 - 0720'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN  721 AND 1080 THEN '0721 - 1080'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN 1081 AND 1800 THEN '1081 - 1800'
WHEN A.ATRASO BETWEEN 1801 AND 3600 THEN '1801 - 3600'
WHEN A.ATRASO > 		3600 		  THEN '3601 - 9999'
ELSE PUT(A.ATRASO,best.) END
;QUIT;




Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_NEUROTECH AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Ult_Atualizacao Format = e8601dt19. Informat e8601dt19. AS Ult_Atualizacao
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.LETR_CONTR
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NOME_COMPLETO
, A.PRI_NOME
, A.GRUPO
, A.CARTEIRA
, A.RISCO
, A.ATRASO
, A.FX_ATRASO
, A.STATUS
, A.COD_ASSESSORIA
, A.ASSESSORIA
, A.FLGFALEC
, A.FLGSUSP
, INPUT(A.DT_NASCIMENTO,ddmmyy10.) Format = yymmdd10. Informat = yymmdd10. AS DT_NASCIMENTO
, A.AQQUE
, A.SEQ_FINANCEIRA
, A.SCORE4
, A.SISTEMA
, A.DMSPDESC
, A.SCORE1
, A.SCORE2
, A.DT_ENVIO
, A.DT_PRI_ATRASO
, A.SALDO_DEVEDOR_VENC
, A.DT_ENQUAD
, A.Fx_Idade
, A.MOD_FIND
, A.PUBLICO
, A.PUBLICO_MKTP
, A.Flag_TipoContr
, A.Flag_TipoProd
, A.Collection
, A.BANDEIRA_CARTAO
, A.VARIANTE_CARTAO
, A.Flag_HU
, A.Volume_HU
, A.Flag_HU_3_6
, A.Flag_HU_4_6
, A.Flag_HU_5_6
, A.Flag_HU_6_6
, A.Flag_Contr_Reneg
, A.Safra_Contr_Reneg
, A.Flag_Canal_Reneg
, A.Flag_Eleg_Reneg
, A.Flag_Wpp_Inter
, A.Flag_Wpp_Insuc
, A.GH_Prop_Digital
, A.Flag_Prop_Digital
, A.Flag_Abd_Site
, A.Flag_Abd_App
, A.Flag_Abd_URA
, A.Flag_Abd_Reneg
, A.Flag_Acess_Site
, A.Flag_Acess_App
, A.Flag_Acess_URA
, A.Flag_Acess_Reneg
, A.Flag_BadBank
, A.Flag_Acao_Contra
, A.Flag_Nexta
, A.Flag_MandadoExp
, A.Flag_Acordo
, A.SEGM_ATIVA
, A.Flag_D0
, A.D0_Atualiz Format = e8601dt19. Informat e8601dt19. AS D0_Atualiz
, A.Inf_Saldo_FGTS
, A.GH_PROP_FGTS
, A.Flag_LiberadoFGTS
, A.Flag_Alo
, A.Flag_CPC
, A.HashBV
, A.Flag_LinhaDig
, A.CdContaCartaoIQ
, A.NuCEP
, A.Cidade
, A.UF
, A.Flag_CA_AtrasoRec

, B.CdFatura
, B.Linha_Digitavel
, DatePart(B.DtCorteFatura) Format = yymmdd10. AS DtCorteFatura
, DatePart(B.DtVencimentoFatura) Format = yymmdd10. AS DtVencimentoFatura
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MaxPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, CASE WHEN B.Max_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Max_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MedPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, CASE WHEN B.Med_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Med_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, B.Vlr_Entrada_MinPlano AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, CASE WHEN B.Min_PlanoParcFatura IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE COMPRESS('1+'||PUT(B.Min_PlanoParcFatura,2.)||'x') END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, CASE WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContrato THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_ElegDesdobro
, CASE WHEN A.NuContratoGestao = D.NuContratoGestao AND D.Flag_ElegPostergar = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS Flag_ElegPostVeic
, E.DDD
, E.Telefone
, E.Telefone_Completo
, F.Email
, G.Agrupamento_Cliente
, G.Agrupamento_Contr
, A.DT_VCTO_PARCELA
, A.NUPARCELA
, A.Flag_PendGravame
, A.DiasUltReceb
, A.Flag_SaidaRisco
, A.DtUltimoRecebimento
, A.CdParceiroComercial
, A.PARCEIRO_COMERCIAL
, A.DESCONTO_QUIT
, A.PROTESTO_STATUS
, A.PROTESTO_DT
, A.TIPO_PESSOA
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_IQ_OFERTA_PARC_FATURA AS B ON A.CdContaCartaoIQ  = B.CdContaCartao
LEFT JOIN WORK.AUX_DESDOBRO 				 AS C ON A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContrato
LEFT JOIN WORK.AUX_DESDOBRO 				 AS D ON A.NuContratoGestao = D.NuContratoGestao
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE 		 AS E ON A.NuCPFCNPJ 		= E.IdPessoa
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.COB_MDM_EMAIL 		 	 AS F ON A.NuCPFCNPJ 		= F.IdPessoa
LEFT JOIN WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_04		 AS G ON A.NuContratoGestao = G.NuContratoGestao
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_nt_contr_rodas as

select distinct
nucpfcnpj
,put(max(nucontratogestao),z14.) as contr_max
from work.aux_neurotech
where Agrupamento_Cliente <> 'Puro'
and Grupo = 'RODAS'
group by nucpfcnpj

;quit;



Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_nt_contr_cartoes as

select distinct
a.nucpfcnpj
,put(max(a.nucontratogestao),z14.) as contr_max
from work.aux_neurotech as a
where a.Agrupamento_Cliente <> 'Puro'
and a.Grupo = 'CARTAO'
and a.nucpfcnpj not in (select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_rodas)
group by a.nucpfcnpj


;quit;



Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_nt_contr_solar as

select distinct
a.nucpfcnpj
,put(max(a.nucontratogestao),z14.) as contr_max
from work.aux_neurotech as a
where a.Agrupamento_Cliente <> 'Puro'
and a.Grupo = 'SOLAR'
and a.nucpfcnpj not in (select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_rodas union all
						select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_cartoes)
group by a.nucpfcnpj


;quit;



Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_nt_contr_cp as

select distinct
a.nucpfcnpj
,put(max(a.nucontratogestao),z14.) as contr_max
from work.aux_neurotech as a
where a.Agrupamento_Cliente <> 'Puro'
and a.Grupo = 'CP'
and a.nucpfcnpj not in (select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_rodas		union all
						select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_cartoes	union all
						select distinct nucpfcnpj from work.aux_nt_contr_solar)
group by a.nucpfcnpj


;quit;



Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_nt_contr as



select * from work.aux_nt_contr_rodas	union all
select * from work.aux_nt_contr_cartoes union all
select * from work.aux_nt_contr_solar	union all
select * from work.aux_nt_contr_cp



;quit;



Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

proc sql;
create table work.aux_neurotech2 as

select distinct
a.*
,case
	when a.Agrupamento_Cliente = 'Puro' then 1
	when put(a.nucontratogestao,z14.) = b.contr_max then 1
	else 0
end as flag_acionamento
from work.aux_neurotech as a
left join work.aux_nt_contr as b on put(a.nucontratogestao,z14.) = b.contr_max

;quit;



proc sql;
create table work.flag_acionamento_nt as

select
NuContratoGestao
,flag_acionamento
from work.aux_neurotech2

;quit;



proc sql;
create table work.sumariz_nt as

select distinct
DT_REF
,grupo
,carteira
,Agrupamento_Cliente
,flag_acionamento
,count(nucontratogestao) as qt
from work.aux_neurotech2
group by
DT_REF
,grupo
,carteira
,Agrupamento_Cliente
,flag_acionamento

;quit;

PROC SQL;

INSERT INTO ZBLOQ.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC
SELECT DISTINCT
  DT_REF
, DATETIME() Format datetime. As Dt_Inclusao
, NUCONTRATOGESTAO Format best14. AS NUCONTRATO
, 1 As Flag_Bloqueio
, 'Migracao_Orquestrador' AS Motivo


FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER_PROD_04
WHERE Agrupamento_Cliente = 'Puro'
AND Agrupamento_Contr IN ('Cartao','Rodas', 'Emprestimos')

;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;






























Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Dt_Inclusao Format = e8601dt19. Informat = e8601dt19.
, A.NUCONTRATO Format = best14. Informat = Best14. AS NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.Flag_Bloqueio
, A.Motivo
FROM ZBLOQ.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS A
WHERE A.DT_REF = TODAY()
AND A.Flag_Bloqueio = 1
AND A.Motivo NOT IN ('Migracao_Orquestrador')
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO_BLOQ_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Motivo
, COUNT(DISTINCT A.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTR
, SUM(A.Flag_Bloqueio) AS QTD_BLOQ
FROM ZBLOQ.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS A
WHERE A.DT_REF = TODAY()
AND A.Flag_Bloqueio = 1

GROUP BY A.DT_REF, A.Motivo
ORDER BY 3 DESC
;QUIT;

DATA _NULL_ ;

RUN;

PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO SYBASEIQ AS DBCON
( HOST     = 'PTA-IQPRD01'
  SERVER   = PTAIQPRD
  DB       = PTAIQCONT
  USER     = &User_PTAIQPRD.
  PASSWORD = &pw_PTAIQPRD.   );
CREATE TABLE WORK.HIST_PGTO_DIGITAL AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  Today() Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ref
, DateTime() Format = DateTime. AS Dt_Atualizacao
, NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, DATEPART(ULT_GERACAO) Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ult_Acordo
, ULT_GERACAO Format = e8601dt19. AS Dth_Ult_Acordo
, DatePart(ULT_DTPAGAMENTO) Format = yymmdd10. AS Dt_Ult_Pgto
FROM CONNECTION TO DBCON(
SELECT DISTINCT
          CON.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
        , MAX(CON.DTGERACAO) AS ULT_GERACAO
        , MAX(CON.DTPAGAMENTO) AS ULT_DTPAGAMENTO
FROM DBCONTENCIOSO.TBNEGOCIACAOCONTRATO CON
WHERE CON.TPENTRADA NOT IN (
  'A'         
, 'C'         
, 'L'         
, 'M'         
, 'R'         
, 'T'         )
AND CON.DTPAGAMENTO IS NOT NULL
AND CON.DTPAGAMENTO  >= DATEADD(DD, -30, GETDATE())
AND CON.DTPAGAMENTO  <= DATEADD(DD,   1, GETDATE())
GROUP BY CON.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
ORDER BY CON.NUCONTRATOGESTAO ASC )
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=Yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_HU_PILOTO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Ult_Atualizacao
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.GRUPO
, A.CARTEIRA
, A.RISCO
, A.ATRASO
, A.FX_ATRASO
, A.Flag_Alo
, A.Flag_CPC
, A.HashBV
, A.PRI_NOME
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
WHERE A.Flag_HU_3_6 = 1
AND A.ATRASO BETWEEN 1 AND 30
AND A.Flag_D0 = 'N'
AND A.FLGFALEC NOT IN ('S')
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=Yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_LEMIT_00 AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
FROM WORK.BASE_HU_PILOTO AS A
LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ, MAX(Interacao) AS Flag_Interacao FROM CONTATOS.AUX_HIST_INTERACAO_WHATS WHERE NUCPFCNPJ IS NOT MISSING AND NUCPFCNPJ NOT IN ('00000000000') GROUP BY NUCPFCNPJ) AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ
WHERE INPUT(SUBSTR(A.NUCPFCNPJ,7,2),best2.) BETWEEN 0 AND 29
AND ((B.Flag_Interacao = 0) OR (B.Flag_Interacao IS MISSING))
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=Yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BASE_LEMIT_01 AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.DT_REF, A.NUCPFCNPJ, B.DDD, B.TELEFONE
FROM WORK.BASE_LEMIT_00 AS A
LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT A.IDPESSOA, A.DDD, A.TELEFONE
FROM (SELECT DISTINCT IDPESSOA, COMPRESS(PUT(DDD,2.)) AS DDD, TELEFONE FROM CONTATOS.AUX_SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL WHERE UPCASE(COMPRESS(dstipotelefone)) = 'CELULAR'
		UNION ALL
	  SELECT DISTINCT IDPESSOA, DDD, TELEFONE FROM CONTATOS.COB_MDM_TELEFONE) AS A
) AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.IDPESSOA
ORDER BY A.NUCPFCNPJ
;QUIT;

INTO :base_cyber SEPARATED BY ' '

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO_CARTEIRA_DIA AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  DT_REF
, CASE
		WHEN ASSESSORIA IN ('FLEX','SERVICES') THEN 'TOLEDO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'CRC' AND GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN 'CRC_FORTALEZA'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'CRC' THEN 'CRC_VINHEDO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO' AND GRUPO = 'RODAS' THEN 'TOLEDO_SAVERIO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO' THEN 'TOLEDO_AVIACAO'
ELSE ASSESSORIA END AS ASSESSORIA
, COUNT(DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ) AS QTD_CPF
, COUNT(DISTINCT NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD_CONTR
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER
GROUP BY DT_REF
, CASE
		WHEN ASSESSORIA IN ('FLEX','SERVICES') THEN 'TOLEDO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'CRC' AND GRUPO = 'CARTAO' THEN 'CRC_FORTALEZA'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'CRC' THEN 'CRC_VINHEDO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO' AND GRUPO = 'RODAS' THEN 'TOLEDO_SAVERIO'
		WHEN ASSESSORIA = 'TOLEDO' THEN 'TOLEDO_AVIACAO'
ELSE ASSESSORIA END
;QUIT;


PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.RESUMO_CARTEIRA_DIA
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/bases_gerais/RESUMO_CARTEIRA_DIA_&DT_ARQ..csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=NO;
 DELIMITER=',';
RUN;

/* TASK: Piloto_INAD_Matriz */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 043 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 147 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: Piloto_INAD_Matriz */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-5f8Lu3uqtbPdTaNU/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00 AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.Flag_MultiplosContr
, A.PRODUTO
, A.Tipo_Contrato
, A.Detalhe_Contrato
, A.FONTE_OFERTA
, A.GRUPO_OFERTA
, A.Telefone_Completo
, A.Dt_Vencimento_Original
, A.ATRASO
, A.Linha_Digitavel
, A.Dt_Max_Pgto
, A.VARIANTE
, A.Vlr_Total_Fatura
, A.Vlr_Minimo_Fatura
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE C.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN C.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(C.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_1

, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE D.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_2
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN D.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(D.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_2

, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE E.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_3
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN E.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(E.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_3

, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE F.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_4
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN F.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(F.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_4

, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE G.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_5
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN G.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(G.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_5

, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN . ELSE H.VrParcelaEntrada 		END AS Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_6
, CASE WHEN B.Max_Parcelamento IS MISSING THEN '' WHEN H.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra IS MISSING THEN '' ELSE '1+'||COMPRESS(PUT(H.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra,2.)) 	END AS Plano_Parcelamento_Fatura_6

, A.NUPARCELA
, A.Valor_Original_Parcela
, 1 AS Carousel_Order
, CASE





	WHEN A.PRODUTO = 'RODAS' 	THEN '750366657829828'
	WHEN A.PRODUTO = 'CARTAO' 	THEN '1078613414042360'
	WHEN A.PRODUTO = 'CP' 		THEN '2354345701625990'
	WHEN A.PRODUTO IN ('SOLAR FLEX','SOLAR GCON','SOLAR') 		THEN '816341984384602'
ELSE 'ProdutoGenerico.png' END AS Carousel_Image
FROM WORK.MATRIZ_INAD AS A
LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT B.NuContratoGestao, MAX(B.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra) AS Max_Parcelamento FROM CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS B GROUP BY B.NuContratoGestao) AS B ON A.NuContratoGestao = B.NuContratoGestao
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS C ON A.NuContratoGestao = C.NuContratoGestao AND C.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 2
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS D ON A.NuContratoGestao = D.NuContratoGestao AND D.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 4
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS E ON A.NuContratoGestao = E.NuContratoGestao AND E.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 6
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS F ON A.NuContratoGestao = F.NuContratoGestao AND F.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 8
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS G ON A.NuContratoGestao = G.NuContratoGestao AND G.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 10
LEFT JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_FATURA_DETAIL AS H ON A.NuContratoGestao = H.NuContratoGestao AND H.CdPlanoVendaPrcloFtra = 12
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;

SELECT DISTINCT PRODUTO, COUNT(*) AS QTD FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00 GROUP BY PRODUTO;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AMOSTRA_JABUTI AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
FROM (SELECT DISTINCT * , Monotonic() AS Ordem, 'Com parcelamento' As Massa
FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00
WHERE Vlr_Total_Fatura > 0
AND Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1 > 0
Having Ordem <= 3

Union All

SELECT DISTINCT * , Monotonic() AS Ordem, 'Sem parcelamento' As Massa
FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00
WHERE Vlr_Total_Fatura > 0
AND Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1 IS MISSING
Having Ordem <= 3

Union All

SELECT DISTINCT * , Monotonic() AS Ordem, 'Min = Saldo Fat + Parc' As Massa
FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00
WHERE Vlr_Total_Fatura > 0
AND Vlr_Total_Fatura = Vlr_Minimo_Fatura
AND Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1 NOT IS MISSING
Having Ordem <= 3

Union All

SELECT DISTINCT * , Monotonic() AS Ordem, 'Min = Saldo Fat - Parc' As Massa
FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00
WHERE Vlr_Total_Fatura > 0
AND Vlr_Total_Fatura = Vlr_Minimo_Fatura
AND Valor_Parcelamento_Fatura_1 IS MISSING
Having Ordem <= 3

Union All

SELECT DISTINCT * , Monotonic() AS Ordem, 'Mult Cartao' As Massa
FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00
WHERE PRODUTO = 'CARTAO'
AND NUCPFCNPJ IN (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ FROM (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ, COUNT(DISTINCT NUCONTRATOGESTAO) AS QTD FROM WORK.INAD_TESTE_MT_00 WHERE PRODUTO = 'CARTAO'  AND Linha_Digitavel  IS NOT MISSING GROUP BY NUCPFCNPJ) WHERE QTD > 1 )
AND Telefone_Completo IS NOT MISSING) AS A

;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AMOSTRA_JABUTI_EXPORT (drop=ORDEM) AS
SELECT DISTINCT * FROM WORK.AMOSTRA_JABUTI
WHERE Ordem <= 6
Order by Massa ASC
;QUIT;

PROC EXPORT
  DATA=WORK.AMOSTRA_JABUTI_EXPORT
  OUTFILE="/riscosbv/cobranca/brsouza/reguas/bases_dia/canais/whatsapp/&DT_ARQ._1_MATRIZ_INAD_JABUTI.csv"
  DBMS=csv
  REPLACE;
  PUTNAMES=YES;
 DELIMITER=';';
RUN;

/* TASK: DB_DebAutomatico */
/* ORDEM EXECUÇÃO: 044 */
/* FLUXO: PFD Flow 147 */
/* ORDEM: Sequencial (Topological Sort) */
/* NOME DO NÓ: DB_DebAutomatico */
/* TIPO: SAS Program */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: CodeTask-1c9mvdrU5lowulMX/code.sas */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
LIBNAME CONTATOS 	'/riscosbv/cobranca/rribeiro/contatos';

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
connect to bigquery as perm (project='bv-sadi-dtm' credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
schema='bv-pdbd-prd' READ_MODE=storage ALLOW_LARGE_RESULTS=must MAX_CHAR_LEN=100);
Create table WORK.DebAut as
Select Distinct
  TODAY() Format=yymmdd10. AS DT_REF
, Datetime() Format = e8601dt19. Informat = e8601dt19. AS Dt_Atualizacao
, cd_conta_cartao As CdContaCartao
, cd_cpf_cnpj As NUCPFCNPJ
, fl_debito_automatico_ativo
, dt_referencia Format = yymmdd10. Informat = yymmdd10. As Dt_Referencia_DA
, dt_adesao_da Format = yymmdd10. Informat = yymmdd10. As Dt_Adesao_DA
from connection to perm(
Select Distinct
  cd_conta_cartao
, cd_cpf_cnpj
, fl_debito_automatico_ativo
, Max(dt_referencia) AS dt_referencia
, Max(dt_adesao_da) AS dt_adesao_da
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_negc_cartao.cartao_debitoautomatico_fato`
GROUP BY cd_conta_cartao, cd_cpf_cnpj, fl_debito_automatico_ativo
);
disconnect from perm;
QUIT;

Options Reuse=Yes Compress=Binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_DEBT_AUT AS
Select Distinct A.*
, CASE
	WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao)) = 10 THEN INPUT(PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao),1,1),best3.),z3.) || PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao),2,9),best10.),z10.),best14.)
	WHEN LENGTH(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao)) = 11 THEN INPUT(PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao),1,2),best3.),z3.) || PUT(INPUT(SUBSTR(COMPRESS(A.CdContaCartao),3,9),best10.),z10.),best14.)
ELSE . END Format = best14. Informat = best14. AS NuContratoGestao
From WORK.DebAut AS A
Inner Join (Select Distinct A.CdContaCartao, A.NUCPFCNPJ, MAX(A.Dt_Referencia_DA) AS Dt_Referencia_DA FROM WORK.DebAut AS A GROUP BY A.CdContaCartao, A.NUCPFCNPJ) AS B ON A.CdContaCartao = B.CdContaCartao AND A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ AND A.Dt_Referencia_DA = B.Dt_Referencia_DA
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=Yes Compress=Binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_DEBT_AUT AS
Select Distinct A.*
FROM WORK.AUX_DEBT_AUT AS A
;QUIT;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Dt_Inclusao Format = e8601dt19. Informat = e8601dt19.
, A.NUCONTRATO Format = best14. Informat = Best14. AS NUCONTRATOGESTAO
, A.Flag_Bloqueio
, A.Motivo
FROM ZBLOQ.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS A
WHERE A.DT_REF = TODAY()
AND A.Flag_Bloqueio = 1
AND A.Motivo NOT IN ('Migracao_Orquestrador')
;QUIT;

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO_BLOQ_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.DT_REF
, A.Motivo
, COUNT(DISTINCT A.NUCONTRATO) AS QTD_CONTR
, SUM(A.Flag_Bloqueio) AS QTD_BLOQ
FROM ZBLOQ.BLOQUEIO_REGUAS_INTERSEC AS A
WHERE A.DT_REF = TODAY()
AND A.Flag_Bloqueio = 1

GROUP BY A.DT_REF, A.Motivo
ORDER BY 3 DESC
;QUIT;

DATA _NULL_ ;
CALL SYMPUT('Hr_Arq', (PUT(HOUR(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)||PUT(MINUTE(intnx('minute',Datetime(),00)),z2.)));
RUN;

PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_INAD AS
SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ
FROM (
SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ FROM WORK.AUX_BASE_CYBER
UNION ALL
SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ FROM WORK.BASE_BASILEIA
UNION ALL
SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ FROM WORK.AUX_IQ_GCON_FLEX)
;QUIT;





options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL ;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM (
project   = 'bv-sadi-dtm'
credfile  = '/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
schema    = 'bv-pdbd-prd'
Read_Mode = Storage
Allow_Large_Results = must
Max_Char_Len = 40);
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_GCP_MDM_TEL AS
SELECT DISTINCT *
from connection to perm (
select distinct
  idbv
, idpessoa
, tppessoa
, rnkdedup
, nuddd
, nutelefone
, nuddi
, cdtipobloqueio
, dstipotelefone
from bv-pdbd-prd.dvry_clie_crm.tbpessoatelefone

)
;disconnect from perm
;QUIT;












options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.AUX_GCP_MDM_TEL_INAD AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.idbv AS HashBV
, COMPRESS(A.idpessoa) AS IdPessoa
, A.tppessoa As Tipo_Pessoa
, A.rnkdedup As Ranking
, A.Nuddd As DDD
, COMPRESS(A.Nutelefone) AS Telefone
, COMPRESS(Cats(A.nuddi, A.nuddd,  A.nutelefone)) As Telefone_Completo
, A.cdtipobloqueio
, A.dstipotelefone
FROM WORK.AUX_GCP_MDM_TEL AS A
INNER JOIN WORK.AUX_INAD  AS B ON A.IDPESSOA = B.NUCPFCNPJ
;QUIT;

options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE CONTATOS.AUX_SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL AS
SELECT DISTINCT *
FROM WORK.AUX_GCP_MDM_TEL_INAD
;QUIT;

options reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
FROM WORK.AUX_GCP_MDM_TEL_INAD AS A
WHERE A.Ranking IN (1,2,3,4,5,6,7)
AND A.Telefone_Completo IS Not Missing

AND SUBSTR(A.Telefone,1,1) = '9'
;quit;




Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP AS
SELECT DISTINCT
TODAY() Format = yymmdd10. AS DT_REF_PROC
, A.DTH AS Ult_CPC
, PUT(A.NuContrato,Best14.) AS CONTRATO
, A.NuContrato
, CATS(A.DDD, A.Numero) AS TelefoneCompleto
, A.DDD
, A.Numero As Telefone
, A.Tipo_Numero AS Tipo_Tel
, 1 AS CPC
, 1 AS Qtd_Ult_CPC
, A.IDPESSOA AS NUCPFCNPJ
FROM WORK.AUX_CPC_INTENALIZ AS A
INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT IDPESSOA, MAX(Dth) AS MAX_DTH FROM WORK.AUX_CPC_INTENALIZ GROUP BY IDPESSOA) AS B ON A.IDPESSOA = B.IDPESSOA AND A.DTH = B.MAX_DTH
INNER JOIN CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS C ON A.NuContrato = C.NUCONTRATOGESTAO
WHERE A.Flag_CPC = 1
AND CATS(A.DDD, A.Numero) NOT IN ('-99')
AND A.Numero IS NOT MISSING
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP_Trat AS
SELECT Distinct
  A.DT_REF_PROC
, A.NUCPFCNPJ
, A.Ult_CPC
, A.TelefoneCompleto
, A.DDD
, A.Telefone
, A.TIPO_TEL
, A.CPC
, A.Qtd_Ult_CPC
FROM WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP AS A
INNER JOIN (Select Distinct A.NUCPFCNPJ, Max(A.Ult_CPC) FORMAT yymmdd10. As Ult_CPC
FROM WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP AS A
Where A.DDD NOT IN ('-9')
AND A.Telefone NOT IN ('9')
AND LENGTH(A.DDD) = 2
AND A.Tipo_Tel = 'CELULAR'
Group By A.DT_REF_PROC, A.NUCPFCNPJ) AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ AND A.Ult_CPC = B.Ult_CPC
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP_Final AS
SELECT DISTINCT A.*
FROM WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP_Trat AS A
INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT NUCPFCNPJ, COUNT(NUCPFCNPJ) AS QTD_CPF FROM WORK.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP_Trat GROUP BY NUCPFCNPJ) AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.NUCPFCNPJ AND B.QTD_CPF = 1
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL_TRAT AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.HashBV
, A.idpessoa
, A.Tipo_Pessoa
, A.Ranking
, PUT(A.DDD, 2.) AS DDD
, A.Telefone
, TRANWRD(Compress(A.Telefone_Completo),".","") As Telefone_Completo
FROM WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL AS A
INNER JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT
  B.idpessoa
, Min(B.Ranking) AS Min_Ranking
FROM WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL AS B
WHERE TRANWRD(Compress(B.Telefone_Completo),".","") NOT IN ('55-99')
AND   TRANWRD(Compress(B.Telefone_Completo),".","") NOT IN (
SELECT TRANWRD(Compress(Telefone_Completo),".","") AS TELEFONE_COMPLETO FROM CONTATOS.AUX_QUARENTENA_TEL
WHERE Flag_BloqueioAtivo = 1
AND   Dt_Expiracao_Bloqueio > TODAY()
) 
GROUP BY B.idpessoa) AS B ON A.idpessoa = B.idpessoa AND A.Ranking = B.Min_Ranking
WHERE A.cdtipobloqueio NOT IN (12,8,10,14,21) 

;QUIT;


Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.QUARENTENA AS
SELECT TRANWRD(Compress(Telefone_Completo),".","") AS TELEFONE_COMPLETO FROM CONTATOS.AUX_QUARENTENA_TEL
WHERE Flag_BloqueioAtivo = 1
AND   Dt_Expiracao_Bloqueio > TODAY()
;QUIT;





Options Reuse=yes compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.HIST_ENR_CPC_DEEP_MDM AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  A.*
, COMPRESS(B.DDD) AS DDD_MDM
, COMPRESS(B.Telefone) AS TELEFONE_MDM
, COMPRESS(CATS(B.DDD,B.Telefone)) AS TEL_MDM
, B.Telefone_Completo AS Tel_Completo_MDM
, B.Ranking

, B.Tipo_Pessoa
, COMPRESS(CATS(C.DDD,C.Telefone)) AS TEL_CPCDEEP
, COMPRESS(C.DDD) AS DDD_CPCDEEP
, COMPRESS(C.Telefone) AS TELEFONE_CPCDEEP
, CASE WHEN COMPRESS(C.Telefone) IS NOT MISSING THEN CATS('55',COMPRESS(C.DDD), COMPRESS(C.Telefone)) ELSE '' END AS Tel_Completo_CPCDEEP
, CASE WHEN A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.IDPESSOA	THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_TEL_MDM
, CASE WHEN A.NUCPFCNPJ = C.NUCPFCNPJ	THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP
, CASE WHEN COMPRESS(CATS(B.DDD,B.Telefone)) IS MISSING AND COMPRESS(CATS(C.DDD,C.Telefone)) IS MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS SEMTEL_MDM_CPCDEEP
, CASE WHEN COMPRESS(CATS(B.DDD,B.Telefone)) IS MISSING AND COMPRESS(CATS(C.DDD,C.Telefone)) IS NOT MISSING THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS STEL_MDM_CTEL_CPCDEEP
, CASE WHEN COMPRESS(CATS(B.DDD,B.Telefone)) IS NOT MISSING AND COMPRESS(CATS(C.DDD,C.Telefone)) IS NOT MISSING AND COMPRESS(CATS(B.DDD,B.Telefone)) = COMPRESS(CATS(C.DDD,C.Telefone)) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS TEL_MDM_CPCDEEP
FROM CONTATOS.AUX_BASE_CYBER AS A
LEFT JOIN WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL_TRAT AS B ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = B.IDPESSOA
LEFT JOIN WORk.HISTORICO_CPC_DEEP_FINAL   AS C ON A.NUCPFCNPJ = C.NUCPFCNPJ 
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.RESUMO AS
SELECT DISTINCT
  DT_REF
, COUNT(NuContratoGestao) As Qtd_Contrato
, COUNT(Distinct NuContratoGestao) As Qtd_Contrato_Uniq
, COUNT(Distinct NUCPFCNPJ) As Qtd_CPF_Uniq
, SUM(FLAG_TEL_MDM) AS TEL_MDM
, SUM(FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP) AS TEL_CPCDEEP
, SUM(SEMTEL_MDM_CPCDEEP) AS STEL_MDM_STEL_CPCDEEP
, SUM(STEL_MDM_CTEL_CPCDEEP) AS STEL_MDM_CTEL_CPCDEEP
, SUM(TEL_MDM_CPCDEEP) AS MDM_CPCDEEP_IGUAIS
FROM WORK.HIST_ENR_CPC_DEEP_MDM
GROUP BY DT_REF
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
CREATE TABLE WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL_TRAT_New AS
SELECT DISTINCT * FROM (SELECT DISTINCT
HashBV
, NUCPFCNPJ AS idpessoa
, Tipo_Pessoa
, Case WHEN FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP = 1 THEN 0 ELSE Ranking END AS Ranking
, Case WHEN FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP = 1 THEN DDD_CPCDEEP ELSE DDD_MDM END AS DDD
, Case WHEN FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP = 1 THEN TELEFONE_CPCDEEP ELSE TELEFONE_MDM END AS Telefone
, Case WHEN FLAG_TEL_CPC_DEEP = 1 THEN Tel_Completo_CPCDEEP ELSE Tel_Completo_MDM END AS Telefone_Completo
FROM WORK.HIST_ENR_CPC_DEEP_MDM) WHERE Telefone_Completo IS NOT MISSING
;QUIT;





PROC SQL;
CREATE INDEX idpessoa ON WORK.SYNAPSE_MDM_TEL_TRAT_New(idpessoa);

Options Reuse=yes Compress=binary;
PROC SQL;
CONNECT TO BIGQUERY AS PERM
(project='bv-sadi-dtm'
credfile='/projetos/acessos/bq/di/sadi-dtm.json'
Schema='bv-pdbd-prd'
Read_Mode=storage

);
CREATE TABLE WORK.BUSCA_APREENSAO AS
SELECT DISTINCT *
FROM CONNECTION TO PERM (
Select Distinct A.*
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbgestaocdccg.tbprocessobuscaapreensao` AS A
INNER JOIN (Select Distinct nucontrato, sqcontratofinanceiro, MAX(cdprocessobuscaapreensao) AS cdprocessobuscaapreensao
FROM `bv-pdbd-prd.trusted_dbgestaocdccg.tbprocessobuscaapreensao`
GROUP BY nucontrato, sqcontratofinanceiro) AS B ON A.nucontrato = B.nucontrato AND A.sqcontratofinanceiro = B.sqcontratofinanceiro AND A.cdprocessobuscaapreensao = B.cdprocessobuscaapreensao
);
 disconnect from PERM
;QUIT;

/* NOME DO NÓ: Embedded Task (XML) */
/* TIPO: Embedded Code (Wizard/Task/Program) */
/* ARQUIVO FONTE: project.xml */
/* ORIGEM: Código persistido em project.xml (embedded / wizard / task) */
/* =========================INÍCIO TASK========================= */
DATA WORK.Aux_CNN;
    LENGTH
        Dt_Ref             8
        Flag_Ativo         8
        System           $ 6
        Instancia        $ 9
        Ref_User         $ 20
        Detail_User      $ 20
        Ref_Pw           $ 20
        Detail_Pw        $ 20 ;
    FORMAT
        Dt_Ref           YYMMDD10.
        Flag_Ativo       BEST1.
        System           $CHAR6.
        Instancia        $CHAR9.
        Ref_User         $CHAR20.
        Detail_User      $CHAR20.
        Ref_Pw           $CHAR20.
        Detail_Pw        $CHAR20. ;
    INFORMAT
        Dt_Ref           YYMMDD10.
        Flag_Ativo       BEST1.
        System           $CHAR6.
        Instancia        $CHAR9.
        Ref_User         $CHAR20.
        Detail_User      $CHAR20.
        Ref_Pw           $CHAR20.
        Detail_Pw        $CHAR20. ;
    INFILE '/saswork/SAS_workC8C600345C9F_srv-sasprd04n1/#LN00011'
        LRECL=82
        ENCODING="LATIN1"
        TERMSTR=CRLF
        DLM='7F'x
        MISSOVER
        DSD ;
    INPUT
        Dt_Ref           : ?? YYMMDD10.
        Flag_Ativo       : ?? BEST1.
        System           : $CHAR6.
        Instancia        : $CHAR9.
        Ref_User         : $CHAR20.
        Detail_User      : $CHAR20.
        Ref_Pw           : $CHAR20.
        Detail_Pw        : $CHAR20. ;
RUN;
